dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eunified\u003c/strong\u003e is a project that transforms content with abstract syntax trees\n(ASTs).\n\u003cstrong\u003erehype\u003c/strong\u003e adds support for HTML to unified.\n\u003cstrong\u003ehast\u003c/strong\u003e is the HTML AST that rehype uses.\nThis is a rehype plugin that transforms hast.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" application by\nnaming elements, through \u003ccode\u003eid\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003ename\u003c/code\u003e attributes, such that they overshadow\npresumed properties in \u003ccode\u003ewindow\u003c/code\u003e (the global scope in browsers).\nDOM clobbering often occurs when user content is used to generate heading IDs.\nTo illustrate, say we have this \u003ccode\u003ebrowser.js\u003c/code\u003e file:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"console.log(current)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003econsole\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003elog\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ecurrent\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAnd our module \u003ccode\u003eexample.js\u003c/code\u003e contains:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"/**\n * @typedef {import('hast').Root} Root\n */\n\nimport fs from 'node:fs/promises'\nimport rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'\nimport rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'\nimport {unified} from 'unified'\n\nconst browser = String(await fs.readFile('browser.js'))\nconst document = `\u0026lt;a name=\u0026quot;old\u0026quot;\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/a\u0026gt;\n\u0026lt;h1 id=\u0026quot;current\u0026quot;\u0026gt;Current\u0026lt;/h1\u0026gt;\n${`\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;${'Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet. '.repeat(20)}\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\\n`.repeat(20)}\n\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;Link to \u0026lt;a href=\u0026quot;#current\u0026quot;\u0026gt;current\u0026lt;/a\u0026gt;, link to \u0026lt;a href=\u0026quot;#old\u0026quot;\u0026gt;old\u0026lt;/a\u0026gt;.`\n\nconst file = await unified()\n  .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})\n  .use(function () {\n    /**\n     * @param {Root} tree\n     */\n    return function (tree) {\n      tree.children.push({\n        type: 'element',\n        tagName: 'script',\n        properties: {type: 'module'},\n        children: [{type: 'text', value: browser}]\n      })\n    }\n  })\n  .use(rehypeStringify)\n  .process(document)\n\nawait fs.writeFile('output.html', String(file))\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan href=\"https://github.com/rehypejs/rehype-highlight\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003erehype-highlight\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e, can be\nsolved similar to how math is solved (see previous example).\nThat is, use \u003ccode\u003erehype-sanitize\u003c/code\u003e and allow the classes needed for highlighting,\nand highlight afterwards:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"import rehypeHighlight from 'rehype-highlight'\nimport rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'\nimport rehypeSanitize, {defaultSchema} from 'rehype-sanitize'\nimport rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'\nimport {unified} from 'unified'\n\nconst file = await unified()\n  .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})\n  .use(rehypeSanitize, {\n    ...defaultSchema,\n    attributes: {\n      ...defaultSchema.attributes,\n      code: [\n        ...(defaultSchema.attributes.code || []),\n        // List of all allowed languages:\n        ['className', 'language-js', 'language-css', 'language-md']\n      ]\n    }\n  })\n  .use(rehypeHighlight, {subset: false})\n  .use(rehypeStringify)\n  .process('\u0026lt;pre\u0026gt;\u0026lt;code className=\u0026quot;language-js\u0026quot;\u0026gt;console.log(1)\u0026lt;/code\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/pre\u0026gt;')\n\nconsole.log(String(file))\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan rehype-sanitize","","[![Build][build-badge]][build]","[![Coverage][coverage-badge]][coverage]","[![Downloads][downloads-badge]][downloads]","[![Size][size-badge]][size]","[![Sponsors][sponsors-badge]][collective]","[![Backers][backers-badge]][collective]","[![Chat][chat-badge]][chat]","","**[rehype][]** plugin to sanitize HTML.","","## Contents","","*   [What is this?](#what-is-this)","*   [When should I use this?](#when-should-i-use-this)","*   [Install](#install)","*   [Use](#use)","*   [API](#api)","    *   [`defaultSchema`](#defaultschema)","    *   [`unified().use(rehypeSanitize[, schema])`](#unifieduserehypesanitize-schema)","    *   [`Options`](#options)","* [License](#license)","","## What is this?","","This package is a [unified][] ([rehype][]) plugin to make sure HTML is safe.","It drops anything that isn’t explicitly allowed by a schema (defaulting to how","`github.com` works).","","**unified** is a project that transforms content with abstract syntax trees","(ASTs).","**rehype** adds support for HTML to unified.","**hast** is the HTML AST that rehype uses.","This is a rehype plugin that transforms hast.","","## When should I use this?","","It’s recommended to sanitize your HTML any time you do not completely trust","authors or the plugins being used.","","This plugin is built on [`hast-util-sanitize`][hast-util-sanitize], which cleans","[hast][] syntax trees.","rehype focusses on making it easier to transform content by abstracting such","internals away.","","## Install","","This package is [ESM only][esm].","In Node.js (version 16+), install with [npm][]:","","```sh","npm install rehype-sanitize","```","","In Deno with [`esm.sh`][esmsh]:","","```js","import rehypeSanitize from 'https://esm.sh/rehype-sanitize@6'","```","","In browsers with [`esm.sh`][esmsh]:","","```html","\u003cscript type=\"module\"\u003e","  import rehypeSanitize from 'https://esm.sh/rehype-sanitize@6?bundle'","\u003c/script\u003e","```","","## Use","","Say we have the following file `index.html`:","","```html","\u003cdiv onmouseover=\"alert('alpha')\"\u003e","  \u003ca href=\"jAva script:alert('bravo')\"\u003edelta\u003c/a\u003e","  \u003cimg src=\"x\" onerror=\"alert('charlie')\"\u003e","  \u003ciframe src=\"javascript:alert('delta')\"\u003e\u003c/iframe\u003e","  \u003cmath\u003e","    \u003cmi xlink:href=\"data:x,\u003cscript\u003ealert('echo')\u003c/script\u003e\"\u003e\u003c/mi\u003e","  \u003c/math\u003e","\u003c/div\u003e","\u003cscript\u003e","require('child_process').spawn('echo', ['hack!']);","\u003c/script\u003e","```","","…and our module `example.js` looks as follows:","","```js","import rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'","import rehypeSanitize from 'rehype-sanitize'","import rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'","import {read} from 'to-vfile'","import {unified} from 'unified'","","const file = await unified()","  .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})","  .use(rehypeSanitize)","  .use(rehypeStringify)","  .process(await read('index.html'))","","console.log(String(file))","```","","Now running `node example.js` yields:","","```html","\u003cdiv\u003e","  \u003ca\u003edelta\u003c/a\u003e","  \u003cimg src=\"x\"\u003e","","","","","\u003c/div\u003e","```","","## API","","This package exports the identifier [`defaultSchema`][api-default-schema].","The default export is [`rehypeSanitize`][api-rehype-sanitize].","","### `defaultSchema`","","Default schema ([`Options`][api-options]).","","Follows GitHub style sanitation.","","### `unified().use(rehypeSanitize[, schema])`","","Sanitize HTML.","","###### Parameters","","*   `options` ([`Options`][api-options], optional)","    — configuration","","###### Returns","","Transform ([`Transformer`][unified-transformer]).","","### `Options`","","Schema that defines what nodes and properties are allowed (TypeScript type).","","This option is a bit advanced as it requires knowledge of syntax trees, so see","the docs for [`Schema` in `hast-util-sanitize`][hast-util-sanitize-schema].","","## Example","","### Example: headings (DOM clobbering)","","DOM clobbering is an attack in which malicious HTML confuses an application by","naming elements, through `id` or `name` attributes, such that they overshadow","presumed properties in `window` (the global scope in browsers).","DOM clobbering often occurs when user content is used to generate heading IDs.","To illustrate, say we have this `browser.js` file:","","```js","console.log(current)","```","","And our module `example.js` contains:","","```js","/**"," * @typedef {import('hast').Root} Root"," */","","import fs from 'node:fs/promises'","import rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'","import rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'","import {unified} from 'unified'","","const browser = String(await fs.readFile('browser.js'))","const document = `\u003ca name=\"old\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e","\u003ch1 id=\"current\"\u003eCurrent\u003c/h1\u003e","${`\u003cp\u003e${'Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet. '.repeat(20)}\u003c/p\u003e\\n`.repeat(20)}","\u003cp\u003eLink to \u003ca href=\"#current\"\u003ecurrent\u003c/a\u003e, link to \u003ca href=\"#old\"\u003eold\u003c/a\u003e.`","","const file = await unified()","  .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})","  .use(function () {","    /**","     * @param {Root} tree","     */","    return function (tree) {","      tree.children.push({","        type: 'element',","        tagName: 'script',","        properties: {type: 'module'},","        children: [{type: 'text', value: browser}]","      })","    }","  })","  .use(rehypeStringify)","  .process(document)","","await fs.writeFile('output.html', String(file))","```","","This code processes HTML, inlines our browser script into it, and writes it out.","The input HTML models how markdown often looks on platforms like GitHub, which","allow heading IDs to be generated from their text and embedded HTML (including","`\u003ca name=\"old\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e`, which can be used to create anchors for renamed headings","to prevent links from breaking).","The generated HTML looks like:","","```html","\u003ca name=\"old\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e","\u003ch1 id=\"current\"\u003eCurrent\u003c/h1\u003e","\u003cp\u003eLorem ipsum dolor sit amet.\u003c!--…--\u003e\u003c/p\u003e","\u003cp\u003eLink to \u003ca href=\"#current\"\u003ecurrent\u003c/a\u003e, link to \u003ca href=\"#old\"\u003eold\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e","\u003cscript type=\"module\"\u003econsole.log(current)\u003c/script\u003e","```","","When you run this code locally and open the generated `output.html`, you can","observe that the links at the bottom work, but also that the `\u003ch1\u003e` element","is printed to the console (the clobbering).","","`rehype-sanitize` solves the clobbering by prefixing every `id` and `name`","attribute with `'user-content-'`.","Changing `example.js`:","","```diff","@@ -15,6 +15,7 @@ ${`\u003cp\u003e${'Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet. '.repeat(20)}\u003c/p\u003e\\n`.repeat(20)}","","   const file = await unified()","     .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})","+    .use(rehypeSanitize)","     .use(function () {","       /**","        * @param {Root} tree","```","","Now yields:","","```diff","-\u003ca name=\"old\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e","-\u003ch1 id=\"current\"\u003eCurrent\u003c/h1\u003e","+\u003ca name=\"user-content-old\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e","+\u003ch1 id=\"user-content-current\"\u003eCurrent\u003c/h1\u003e","```","","This introduces another problem as the links are now broken.","It could perhaps be solved by changing all links, but that would make the links","rather ugly, and we’d need to track what IDs we have outside of the user content","on our pages too.","Alternatively, and what arguably looks better, we could rewrite pretty links to","their safe but ugly prefixed elements.","This is what GitHub does.","Replace `browser.js` with the following:","","```js","/// \u003creference lib=\"dom\" /\u003e","/* eslint-env browser */","","// Page load (you could wrap this in a DOM ready if the script is loaded early).","hashchange()","","// When URL changes.","window.addEventListener('hashchange', hashchange)","","// When on the URL already, perhaps after scrolling, and clicking again, which","// doesn’t emit `hashchange`.","document.addEventListener(","  'click',","  function (event) {","    if (","      event.target \u0026\u0026","      event.target instanceof HTMLAnchorElement \u0026\u0026","      event.target.href === location.href \u0026\u0026","      location.hash.length \u003e 1","    ) {","      setImmediate(function () {","        if (!event.defaultPrevented) {","          hashchange()","        }","      })","    }","  },","  false",")","","function hashchange() {","  /** @type {string | undefined} */","  let hash","","  try {","    hash = decodeURIComponent(location.hash.slice(1)).toLowerCase()","  } catch {","    return","  }","","  const name = 'user-content-' + hash","  const target =","    document.getElementById(name) || document.getElementsByName(name)[0]","","  if (target) {","    setImmediate(function () {","      target.scrollIntoView()","    })","  }","}","```","","### Example: math","","Math can be enabled in rehype by using the plugins","[`rehype-katex`][rehype-katex] or [`rehype-mathjax`][rehype-mathjax].","The operate on elements with certain classes and inject complex markup and of","inline styles, most of which this plugin will remove.","Say our module `example.js` contains:","","```js","import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex'","import rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'","import rehypeSanitize from 'rehype-sanitize'","import rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'","import {unified} from 'unified'","","const file = await unified()","  .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})","  .use(rehypeKatex)","  .use(rehypeSanitize)","  .use(rehypeStringify)","  .process('\u003ccode class=\"math-inline\"\u003eL\u003c/code\u003e')","","console.log(String(file))","```","","Running that yields:","","```html","\u003cspan\u003e\u003cspan\u003eLL\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u003cspan\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan\u003eL\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","```","","It is possible to pass a schema which allows MathML and inline styles, but it","would be complex and allows *all* inline styles, which is unsafe.","Alternatively, and arguably better, would be to *first* sanitize the HTML,","allowing only the specific classes that `rehype-katex` and `rehype-mathjax` use,","and *then* using those plugins:","","```diff","@@ -1,13 +1,20 @@"," import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex'"," import rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'","-import rehypeSanitize from 'rehype-sanitize'","+import rehypeSanitize, {defaultSchema} from 'rehype-sanitize'"," import rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'"," import {unified} from 'unified'",""," const file = await unified()","   .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})","+  .use(rehypeSanitize, {","+    ...defaultSchema,","+    attributes: {","+      ...defaultSchema.attributes,","+      // The `language-*` regex is allowed by default.","+      code: [['className', /^language-./, 'math-inline', 'math-display']]","+    }","+  })","   .use(rehypeKatex)","-  .use(rehypeSanitize)","   .use(rehypeStringify)","   .process('\u003ccode class=\"math-inline\"\u003eL\u003c/code\u003e')","```","","Running that yields:","","```html","\u003cspan class=\"katex\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"katex-mathml\"\u003e\u003cmath xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML\"\u003e…\u003c/math\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"katex-html\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e…\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","```","","### Example: syntax highlighting","","Highlighting, for example with [`rehype-highlight`][rehype-highlight], can be","solved similar to how math is solved (see previous example).","That is, use `rehype-sanitize` and allow the classes needed for highlighting,","and highlight afterwards:","","```js","import rehypeHighlight from 'rehype-highlight'","import rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'","import rehypeSanitize, {defaultSchema} from 'rehype-sanitize'","import rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'","import {unified} from 'unified'","","const file = await unified()","  .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})","  .use(rehypeSanitize, {","    ...defaultSchema,","    attributes: {","      ...defaultSchema.attributes,","      code: [","        ...(defaultSchema.attributes.code || []),","        // List of all allowed languages:","        ['className', 'language-js', 'language-css', 'language-md']","      ]","    }","  })","  .use(rehypeHighlight, {subset: false})","  .use(rehypeStringify)","  .process('\u003cpre\u003e\u003ccode className=\"language-js\"\u003econsole.log(1)\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e')","","console.log(String(file))","```","","Alternatively, it’s possible to make highlighting safe by allowing all the","classes used on tokens.","Modifying the above code like so:","","```diff"," const file = await unified()","   .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})","+  .use(rehypeHighlight, {subset: false})","   .use(rehypeSanitize, {","     ...defaultSchema,","     attributes: {","       ...defaultSchema.attributes,","-      code: [","-        ...(defaultSchema.attributes.code || []),","-        // List of all allowed languages:","-        ['className', 'hljs', 'language-js', 'language-css', 'language-md']","+      span: [","+        ...(defaultSchema.attributes.span || []),","+        // List of all allowed tokens:","+        ['className', 'hljs-addition', 'hljs-attr', 'hljs-attribute', 'hljs-built_in', 'hljs-bullet', 'hljs-char', 'hljs-code', 'hljs-comment', 'hljs-deletion', 'hljs-doctag', 'hljs-emphasis', 'hljs-formula', 'hljs-keyword', 'hljs-link', 'hljs-literal', 'hljs-meta', 'hljs-name', 'hljs-number', 'hljs-operator', 'hljs-params', 'hljs-property', 'hljs-punctuation', 'hljs-quote', 'hljs-regexp', 'hljs-section', 'hljs-selector-attr', 'hljs-selector-class', 'hljs-selector-id', 'hljs-selector-pseudo', 'hljs-selector-tag', 'hljs-string', 'hljs-strong', 'hljs-subst', 'hljs-symbol', 'hljs-tag', 'hljs-template-tag', 'hljs-template-variable', 'hljs-title', 'hljs-type', 'hljs-variable'","+          ]","       ]","     }","   })","-  .use(rehypeHighlight, {subset: false})","   .use(rehypeStringify)","   .process('\u003cpre\u003e\u003ccode className=\"language-js\"\u003econsole.log(1)\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e')","```","","## Types","","This package is fully typed with [TypeScript][].","It exports the additional type [`Options`][api-options].","","## Compatibility","","Projects maintained by the unified collective are compatible with maintained","versions of Node.js.","","When we cut a new major release, we drop support for unmaintained versions of","Node.","This means we try to keep the current release line, `rehype-sanitize@^6`,","compatible with Node.js 16.","","This plugin works with `rehype-parse` version 3+, `rehype-stringify` version 3+,","`rehype` version 5+, and `unified` version 6+.","","## Security","","The defaults are safe but improper use of `rehype-sanitize` can open you up to a","[cross-site scripting (XSS)][xss] attack.","","Use `rehype-sanitize` after the last unsafe thing: everything after","`rehype-sanitize` could be unsafe (but is fine if you do trust it).","","## Related","","*   [`hast-util-sanitize`](https://github.com/syntax-tree/hast-util-sanitize)","    — utility to sanitize [hast][]","*   [`rehype-format`](https://github.com/rehypejs/rehype-format)","    — format HTML","*   [`rehype-minify`](https://github.com/rehypejs/rehype-minify)","    — minify HTML","","## Contribute","","See [`contributing.md`][contributing] in [`rehypejs/.github`][health] for ways","to get started.","See [`support.md`][support] for ways to get help.","","This project has <p dir="auto"><strong><a href="https://github.com/rehypejs/rehype">rehype</a></strong> plugin to sanitize HTML.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contents</h2><a id="user-content-contents" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contents" href="#contents"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What is this?</h2><a id="user-content-what-is-this" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What is this?" href="#what-is-this"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This package is a <a href="https://github.com/unifiedjs/unified">unified</a> (<a href="https://github.com/rehypejs/rehype">rehype</a>) plugin to make sure HTML is safe.
It drops anything that isn’t explicitly allowed by a schema (defaulting to how
<code>github.com</code> works).</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>unified</strong> is a project that transforms content with abstract syntax trees
(ASTs).
<strong>rehype</strong> adds support for HTML to unified.
<strong>hast</strong> is the HTML AST that rehype uses.
This is a rehype plugin that transforms hast.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">When should I use this?</h2><a id="user-content-when-should-i-use-this" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: When should I use this?" href="#when-should-i-use-this"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">It’s recommended to sanitize your HTML any time you do not completely trust
authors or the plugins being used.</p>
<p dir="auto">This plugin is built on <a href="https://github.com/syntax-tree/hast-util-sanitize"><code>hast-util-sanitize</code></a>, which cleans
<a href="https://github.com/syntax-tree/hast">hast</a> syntax trees.
rehype focusses on making it easier to transform content by abstracting such
internals away.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Install</h2><a id="user-content-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Install" href="#install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This package is <a href="https://gist.github.com/sindresorhus/a39789f98801d908bbc7ff3ecc99d99c">ESM only</a>.
In Node.js (version 16+), install with <a href="https://docs.npmjs.com/cli/install" rel="nofollow">npm</a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install rehype-sanitize"><pre>npm install rehype-sanitize</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">In Deno with <a href="https://esm.sh" rel="nofollow"><code>esm.sh</code></a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import rehypeSanitize from 'https://esm.sh/rehype-sanitize@6'"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeSanitize</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'https://esm.sh/rehype-sanitize@6'</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">In browsers with <a href="https://esm.sh" rel="nofollow"><code>esm.sh</code></a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;script type=&quot;module&quot;&gt;
  import rehypeSanitize from 'https://esm.sh/rehype-sanitize@6?bundle'
&lt;/script&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">script</span> <span class="pl-c1">type</span>="<span class="pl-s">module</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeSanitize</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'https://esm.sh/rehype-sanitize@6?bundle'</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">script</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Use</h2><a id="user-content-use" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Use" href="#use"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Say we have the following file <code>index.html</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div onmouseover=&quot;alert('alpha')&quot;&gt;
  &lt;a href=&quot;jAva script:alert('bravo')&quot;&gt;delta&lt;/a&gt;
  &lt;img src=&quot;x&quot; onerror=&quot;alert('charlie')&quot;&gt;
  &lt;iframe src=&quot;javascript:alert('delta')&quot;&gt;&lt;/iframe&gt;
  &lt;math&gt;
    &lt;mi xlink:href=&quot;data:x,&lt;script&gt;alert('echo')&lt;/script&gt;&quot;&gt;&lt;/mi&gt;
  &lt;/math&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;script&gt;
require('child_process').spawn('echo', ['hack!']);
&lt;/script&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">onmouseover</span>="<span class="pl-s">alert('alpha')</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">a</span> <span class="pl-c1">href</span>="<span class="pl-s">jAva script:alert('bravo')</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>delta<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">a</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">img</span> <span class="pl-c1">src</span>="<span class="pl-s">x</span>" <span class="pl-c1">onerror</span>="<span class="pl-s">alert('charlie')</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">iframe</span> <span class="pl-c1">src</span>="<span class="pl-s">javascript:alert('delta')</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">iframe</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">math</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">mi</span> <span class="pl-c1">xlink:href</span>="<span class="pl-s">data:x,&lt;script&gt;alert('echo')&lt;/script&gt;</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">mi</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">math</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">script</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-en">require</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'child_process'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">spawn</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'echo'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">'hack!'</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">script</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">…and our module <code>example.js</code> looks as follows:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'
import rehypeSanitize from 'rehype-sanitize'
import rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'
import {read} from 'to-vfile'
import {unified} from 'unified'

const file = await unified()
  .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})
  .use(rehypeSanitize)
  .use(rehypeStringify)
  .process(await read('index.html'))

console.log(String(file))"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeParse</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-parse'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeSanitize</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-sanitize'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeStringify</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-stringify'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-s1">read</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'to-vfile'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-s1">unified</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'unified'</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">file</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-en">unified</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeParse</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-c1">fragment</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeSanitize</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeStringify</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">process</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-en">read</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'index.html'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-v">String</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Now running <code>node example.js</code> yields:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div&gt;
  &lt;a&gt;delta&lt;/a&gt;
  &lt;img src=&quot;x&quot;&gt;




&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">a</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>delta<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">a</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">img</span> <span class="pl-c1">src</span>="<span class="pl-s">x</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>




<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">API</h2><a id="user-content-api" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: API" href="#api"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This package exports the identifier <a href="#defaultschema"><code>defaultSchema</code></a>.
The default export is <a href="#unifieduserehypesanitize-schema"><code>rehypeSanitize</code></a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>defaultSchema</code></h3><a id="user-content-defaultschema" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: defaultSchema" href="#defaultschema"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Default schema (<a href="#options"><code>Options</code></a>).</p>
<p dir="auto">Follows GitHub style sanitation.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>unified().use(rehypeSanitize[, schema])</code></h3><a id="user-content-unifieduserehypesanitize-schema" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: unified().use(rehypeSanitize[, schema])" href="#unifieduserehypesanitize-schema"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Sanitize HTML.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h6 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Parameters</h6><a id="user-content-parameters" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Parameters" href="#parameters"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>options</code> (<a href="#options"><code>Options</code></a>, optional)
— configuration</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h6 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Returns</h6><a id="user-content-returns" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Returns" href="#returns"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Transform (<a href="https://github.com/unifiedjs/unified#transformer"><code>Transformer</code></a>).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>Options</code></h3><a id="user-content-options" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Options" href="#options"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Schema that defines what nodes and properties are allowed (TypeScript type).</p>
<p dir="auto">This option is a bit advanced as it requires knowledge of syntax trees, so see
the docs for <a href="https://github.com/syntax-tree/hast-util-sanitize#schema"><code>Schema</code> in <code>hast-util-sanitize</code></a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example</h2><a id="user-content-example" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example" href="#example"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example: headings (DOM clobbering)</h3><a id="user-content-example-headings-dom-clobbering" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example: headings (DOM clobbering)" href="#example-headings-dom-clobbering"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">DOM clobbering is an attack in which malicious HTML confuses an application by
naming elements, through <code>id</code> or <code>name</code> attributes, such that they overshadow
presumed properties in <code>window</code> (the global scope in browsers).
DOM clobbering often occurs when user content is used to generate heading IDs.
To illustrate, say we have this <code>browser.js</code> file:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="console.log(current)"><pre><span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">current</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">And our module <code>example.js</code> contains:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/**
 * @typedef {import('hast').Root} Root
 */

import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'
import rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'
import {unified} from 'unified'

const browser = String(await fs.readFile('browser.js'))
const document = `&lt;a name=&quot;old&quot;&gt;&lt;/a&gt;
&lt;h1 id=&quot;current&quot;&gt;Current&lt;/h1&gt;
${`&lt;p&gt;${'Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet. '.repeat(20)}&lt;/p&gt;\n`.repeat(20)}
&lt;p&gt;Link to &lt;a href=&quot;#current&quot;&gt;current&lt;/a&gt;, link to &lt;a href=&quot;#old&quot;&gt;old&lt;/a&gt;.`

const file = await unified()
  .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})
  .use(function () {
    /**
     * @param {Root} tree
     */
    return function (tree) {
      tree.children.push({
        type: 'element',
        tagName: 'script',
        properties: {type: 'module'},
        children: [{type: 'text', value: browser}]
      })
    }
  })
  .use(rehypeStringify)
  .process(document)

await fs.writeFile('output.html', String(file))"><pre><span class="pl-c">/**</span>
<span class="pl-c"> * <span class="pl-k">@typedef</span> {<span class="pl-smi">import('hast').Root</span>} Root</span>
<span class="pl-c"> */</span>

<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">fs</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'node:fs/promises'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeParse</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-parse'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeStringify</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-stringify'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-s1">unified</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'unified'</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">browser</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">String</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">fs</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">readFile</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'browser.js'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">document</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">`&lt;a name="old"&gt;&lt;/a&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-s">&lt;h1 id="current"&gt;Current&lt;/h1&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s">`&lt;p&gt;<span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s">'Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet. '</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">repeat</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">20</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>&lt;/p&gt;\n`</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">repeat</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">20</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span></span>
<span class="pl-s">&lt;p&gt;Link to &lt;a href="#current"&gt;current&lt;/a&gt;, link to &lt;a href="#old"&gt;old&lt;/a&gt;.`</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">file</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-en">unified</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeParse</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-c1">fragment</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-k">function</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c">/**</span>
<span class="pl-c">     * <span class="pl-k">@param</span> {<span class="pl-smi">Root</span>} tree</span>
<span class="pl-c">     */</span>
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-k">function</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">tree</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-s1">tree</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">children</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">push</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
        <span class="pl-c1">type</span>: <span class="pl-s">'element'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
        <span class="pl-c1">tagName</span>: <span class="pl-s">'script'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
        <span class="pl-c1">properties</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-c1">type</span>: <span class="pl-s">'module'</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
        <span class="pl-c1">children</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-c1">type</span>: <span class="pl-s">'text'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">value</span>: <span class="pl-s1">browser</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span>
      <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeStringify</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">process</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">document</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">fs</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">writeFile</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'output.html'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-v">String</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This code processes HTML, inlines our browser script into it, and writes it out.
The input HTML models how markdown often looks on platforms like GitHub, which
allow heading IDs to be generated from their text and embedded HTML (including
<code>&lt;a name="old"&gt;&lt;/a&gt;</code>, which can be used to create anchors for renamed headings
to prevent links from breaking).
The generated HTML looks like:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;a name=&quot;old&quot;&gt;&lt;/a&gt;
&lt;h1 id=&quot;current&quot;&gt;Current&lt;/h1&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet.&lt;!--…--&gt;&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Link to &lt;a href=&quot;#current&quot;&gt;current&lt;/a&gt;, link to &lt;a href=&quot;#old&quot;&gt;old&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;script type=&quot;module&quot;&gt;console.log(current)&lt;/script&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">a</span> <span class="pl-c1">name</span>="<span class="pl-s">old</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">a</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">h1</span> <span class="pl-c1">id</span>="<span class="pl-s">current</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Current<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">h1</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">p</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet.<span class="pl-c">&lt;!--…--&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">p</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">p</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Link to <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">a</span> <span class="pl-c1">href</span>="<span class="pl-s">#current</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>current<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">a</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>, link to <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">a</span> <span class="pl-c1">href</span>="<span class="pl-s">#old</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>old<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">a</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>.<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">p</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">script</span> <span class="pl-c1">type</span>="<span class="pl-s">module</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">current</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">script</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">When you run this code locally and open the generated <code>output.html</code>, you can
observe that the links at the bottom work, but also that the <code>&lt;h1&gt;</code> element
is printed to the console (the clobbering).</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>rehype-sanitize</code> solves the clobbering by prefixing every <code>id</code> and <code>name</code>
attribute with <code>'user-content-'</code>.
Changing <code>example.js</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-diff notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@@ -15,6 +15,7 @@ ${`&lt;p&gt;${'Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet. '.repeat(20)}&lt;/p&gt;\n`.repeat(20)}

   const file = await unified()
     .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})
+    .use(rehypeSanitize)
     .use(function () {
       /**
        * @param {Root} tree"><pre><span class="pl-mdr">@@ -15,6 +15,7 @@</span> ${`&lt;p&gt;${'Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet. '.repeat(20)}&lt;/p&gt;\n`.repeat(20)}

   const file = await unified()
     .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})
<span class="pl-mi1"><span class="pl-mi1">+</span>    .use(rehypeSanitize)</span>
     .use(function () {
       /**
        * @param {Root} tree</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Now yields:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-diff notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="-&lt;a name=&quot;old&quot;&gt;&lt;/a&gt;
-&lt;h1 id=&quot;current&quot;&gt;Current&lt;/h1&gt;
+&lt;a name=&quot;user-content-old&quot;&gt;&lt;/a&gt;
+&lt;h1 id=&quot;user-content-current&quot;&gt;Current&lt;/h1&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-md"><span class="pl-md">-</span>&lt;a name="old"&gt;&lt;/a&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-md"><span class="pl-md">-</span>&lt;h1 id="current"&gt;Current&lt;/h1&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-mi1"><span class="pl-mi1">+</span>&lt;a name="user-content-old"&gt;&lt;/a&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-mi1"><span class="pl-mi1">+</span>&lt;h1 id="user-content-current"&gt;Current&lt;/h1&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This introduces another problem as the links are now broken.
It could perhaps be solved by changing all links, but that would make the links
rather ugly, and we’d need to track what IDs we have outside of the user content
on our pages too.
Alternatively, and what arguably looks better, we could rewrite pretty links to
their safe but ugly prefixed elements.
This is what GitHub does.
Replace <code>browser.js</code> with the following:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/// &lt;reference lib=&quot;dom&quot; /&gt;
/* eslint-env browser */

// Page load (you could wrap this in a DOM ready if the script is loaded early).
hashchange()

// When URL changes.
window.addEventListener('hashchange', hashchange)

// When on the URL already, perhaps after scrolling, and clicking again, which
// doesn’t emit `hashchange`.
document.addEventListener(
  'click',
  function (event) {
    if (
      event.target &amp;&amp;
      event.target instanceof HTMLAnchorElement &amp;&amp;
      event.target.href === location.href &amp;&amp;
      location.hash.length &gt; 1
    ) {
      setImmediate(function () {
        if (!event.defaultPrevented) {
          hashchange()
        }
      })
    }
  },
  false
)

function hashchange() {
  /** @type {string | undefined} */
  let hash

  try {
    hash = decodeURIComponent(location.hash.slice(1)).toLowerCase()
  } catch {
    return
  }

  const name = 'user-content-' + hash
  const target =
    document.getElementById(name) || document.getElementsByName(name)[0]

  if (target) {
    setImmediate(function () {
      target.scrollIntoView()
    })
  }
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">/// &lt;reference lib="dom" /&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c">/* eslint-env browser */</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Page load (you could wrap this in a DOM ready if the script is loaded early).</span>
<span class="pl-en">hashchange</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// When URL changes.</span>
<span class="pl-smi">window</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">addEventListener</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'hashchange'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">hashchange</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-c">// When on the URL already, perhaps after scrolling, and clicking again, which</span>
<span class="pl-c">// doesn’t emit `hashchange`.</span>
<span class="pl-smi">document</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">addEventListener</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span>
  <span class="pl-s">'click'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-k">function</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">event</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span>
      <span class="pl-s1">event</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">target</span> <span class="pl-c1">&amp;&amp;</span>
      <span class="pl-s1">event</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">target</span> <span class="pl-k">instanceof</span> <span class="pl-v">HTMLAnchorElement</span> <span class="pl-c1">&amp;&amp;</span>
      <span class="pl-s1">event</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">target</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">href</span> <span class="pl-c1">===</span> <span class="pl-s1">location</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">href</span> <span class="pl-c1">&amp;&amp;</span>
      <span class="pl-s1">location</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">hash</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">length</span> <span class="pl-c1">&gt;</span> <span class="pl-c1">1</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-en">setImmediate</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-k">function</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
        <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">!</span><span class="pl-s1">event</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">defaultPrevented</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
          <span class="pl-en">hashchange</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
        <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
      <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">false</span>
<span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-k">function</span> <span class="pl-en">hashchange</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">/** <span class="pl-k">@type</span> {<span class="pl-smi">string | undefined</span>} */</span>
  <span class="pl-k">let</span> <span class="pl-s1">hash</span>

  <span class="pl-k">try</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">hash</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">decodeURIComponent</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">location</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">hash</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">slice</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toLowerCase</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">catch</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-k">return</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>

  <span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">name</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">'user-content-'</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-s1">hash</span>
  <span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">target</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span>
    <span class="pl-smi">document</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">getElementById</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">name</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">||</span> <span class="pl-smi">document</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">getElementsByName</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">name</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span>

  <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">target</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-en">setImmediate</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-k">function</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-s1">target</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">scrollIntoView</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example: math</h3><a id="user-content-example-math" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example: math" href="#example-math"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Math can be enabled in rehype by using the plugins
<a href="https://github.com/remarkjs/remark-math/tree/main/packages/rehype-katex"><code>rehype-katex</code></a> or <a href="https://github.com/remarkjs/remark-math/tree/main/packages/rehype-mathjax"><code>rehype-mathjax</code></a>.
The operate on elements with certain classes and inject complex markup and of
inline styles, most of which this plugin will remove.
Say our module <code>example.js</code> contains:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex'
import rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'
import rehypeSanitize from 'rehype-sanitize'
import rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'
import {unified} from 'unified'

const file = await unified()
  .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})
  .use(rehypeKatex)
  .use(rehypeSanitize)
  .use(rehypeStringify)
  .process('&lt;code class=&quot;math-inline&quot;&gt;L&lt;/code&gt;')

console.log(String(file))"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeKatex</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-katex'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeParse</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-parse'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeSanitize</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-sanitize'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeStringify</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-stringify'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-s1">unified</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'unified'</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">file</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-en">unified</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeParse</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-c1">fragment</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeKatex</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeSanitize</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeStringify</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">process</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'&lt;code class="math-inline"&gt;L&lt;/code&gt;'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-v">String</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Running that yields:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;LL&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;L&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>LL<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>L<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">It is possible to pass a schema which allows MathML and inline styles, but it
would be complex and allows <em>all</em> inline styles, which is unsafe.
Alternatively, and arguably better, would be to <em>first</em> sanitize the HTML,
allowing only the specific classes that <code>rehype-katex</code> and <code>rehype-mathjax</code> use,
and <em>then</em> using those plugins:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-diff notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@@ -1,13 +1,20 @@
 import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex'
 import rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'
-import rehypeSanitize from 'rehype-sanitize'
+import rehypeSanitize, {defaultSchema} from 'rehype-sanitize'
 import rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'
 import {unified} from 'unified'

 const file = await unified()
   .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})
+  .use(rehypeSanitize, {
+    ...defaultSchema,
+    attributes: {
+      ...defaultSchema.attributes,
+      // The `language-*` regex is allowed by default.
+      code: [['className', /^language-./, 'math-inline', 'math-display']]
+    }
+  })
   .use(rehypeKatex)
-  .use(rehypeSanitize)
   .use(rehypeStringify)
   .process('&lt;code class=&quot;math-inline&quot;&gt;L&lt;/code&gt;')"><pre><span class="pl-mdr">@@ -1,13 +1,20 @@</span>
 import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex'
 import rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'
<span class="pl-md"><span class="pl-md">-</span>import rehypeSanitize from 'rehype-sanitize'</span>
<span class="pl-mi1"><span class="pl-mi1">+</span>import rehypeSanitize, {defaultSchema} from 'rehype-sanitize'</span>
 import rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'
 import {unified} from 'unified'

 const file = await unified()
   .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})
<span class="pl-mi1"><span class="pl-mi1">+</span>  .use(rehypeSanitize, {</span>
<span class="pl-mi1"><span class="pl-mi1">+</span>    ...defaultSchema,</span>
<span class="pl-mi1"><span class="pl-mi1">+</span>    attributes: {</span>
<span class="pl-mi1"><span class="pl-mi1">+</span>      ...defaultSchema.attributes,</span>
<span class="pl-mi1"><span class="pl-mi1">+</span>      // The `language-*` regex is allowed by default.</span>
<span class="pl-mi1"><span class="pl-mi1">+</span>      code: [['className', /^language-./, 'math-inline', 'math-display']]</span>
<span class="pl-mi1"><span class="pl-mi1">+</span>    }</span>
<span class="pl-mi1"><span class="pl-mi1">+</span>  })</span>
   .use(rehypeKatex)
<span class="pl-md"><span class="pl-md">-</span>  .use(rehypeSanitize)</span>
   .use(rehypeStringify)
   .process('&lt;code class="math-inline"&gt;L&lt;/code&gt;')</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Running that yields:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;span class=&quot;katex&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;katex-mathml&quot;&gt;&lt;math xmlns=&quot;http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML&quot;&gt;…&lt;/math&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;katex-html&quot; aria-hidden=&quot;true&quot;&gt;…&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">katex</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">katex-mathml</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">math</span> <span class="pl-c1">xmlns</span>="<span class="pl-s">http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>…<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">math</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">katex-html</span>" <span class="pl-c1">aria-hidden</span>="<span class="pl-s">true</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>…<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">span</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example: syntax highlighting</h3><a id="user-content-example-syntax-highlighting" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example: syntax highlighting" href="#example-syntax-highlighting"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Highlighting, for example with <a href="https://github.com/rehypejs/rehype-highlight"><code>rehype-highlight</code></a>, can be
solved similar to how math is solved (see previous example).
That is, use <code>rehype-sanitize</code> and allow the classes needed for highlighting,
and highlight afterwards:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import rehypeHighlight from 'rehype-highlight'
import rehypeParse from 'rehype-parse'
import rehypeSanitize, {defaultSchema} from 'rehype-sanitize'
import rehypeStringify from 'rehype-stringify'
import {unified} from 'unified'

const file = await unified()
  .use(rehypeParse, {fragment: true})
  .use(rehypeSanitize, {
    ...defaultSchema,
    attributes: {
      ...defaultSchema.attributes,
      code: [
        ...(defaultSchema.attributes.code || []),
        // List of all allowed languages:
        ['className', 'language-js', 'language-css', 'language-md']
      ]
    }
  })
  .use(rehypeHighlight, {subset: false})
  .use(rehypeStringify)
  .process('&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code className=&quot;language-js&quot;&gt;console.log(1)&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;')

console.log(String(file))"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeHighlight</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-highlight'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeParse</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-parse'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeSanitize</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-s1">defaultSchema</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-sanitize'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">rehypeStringify</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'rehype-stringify'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-s1">unified</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'unified'</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">file</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-en">unified</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeParse</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-c1">fragment</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeSanitize</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    ...<span class="pl-s1">defaultSchema</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">attributes</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      ...<span class="pl-s1">defaultSchema</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">attributes</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
      <span class="pl-c1">code</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span>
        ...<span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">defaultSchema</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">attributes</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">code</span> <span class="pl-c1">||</span> <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
        <span class="pl-c">// List of all allowed languages:</span>
        <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">'className'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">'language-js'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">'language-css'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">'language-md'</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span>
      <span class="pl-kos">]</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeHighlight</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-c1">subset</span>: <span class="pl-c1">false</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">use</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">rehypeStringify</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">process</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code className="language-js"&gt;console.log(1)&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>

<span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-v">String</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">file</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Alternatively, it’s possible to make highlighting safe by allowing all the
classes used on tokens.
Modifying the above code like so:</p>

+          ]
